# WORKLOG — what changed, where, why, when, which commit

Append-only. One entry per real commit, generated by
`node scripts/log-change.cjs` (never hand-typed) so the hash, files and UTC
time cannot drift from reality. Newest entries go at the end. A commit that
only appends to this file is exempt from needing an entry.

```
node scripts/log-change.cjs --layer L2 --why "why it had to change" \
  --evidence "tests 11/11, OVERLAY_OK, event living-haul-cargo-adopted"
node scripts/log-change.cjs --check     # format + duplicate audit
```

Layers: `L1` stock EveJS patch, `L2` co-op overlay, `L3` new mechanism,
`DOCS`, `TEST`, `OPS`. Full rules: `docs/UPSTREAM_PATCH_SERIES.md`,
`docs/DEVELOPMENT_DOCTRINE.md`.

---

### 2026-10-26T10:34Z · 1d8330d · L2 · fix(station): accept the trip dwell proof alongside the live arrival record

Why: an in-memory arrivals record lost across a trip rebuild blocked every sale with ARRIVAL_NOT_VERIFIED, so ore reached the station but never became ISK.
Where: integration/coopNativeStationArrival.cjs
Evidence: tests/coop-native-station-arrival.test.mjs 15/15; restarted server, receipts 18 -> 18; sale still unproven at that point.

### 2026-10-26T15:04Z · 775486a · L1 · feat(patches): own the atomic upstream patch series; persist transient haul cargo for restart adoption

Why: every native change lived only as a string edit inside the checkout, and transient haulers never persisted their cargo, so a restart lost the ore. Both made restarts start the fleet from zero.
Where: patches/upstream/0001, patches/upstream/0002, patches/upstream/MANIFEST.json, scripts/apply-upstream-patches.cjs, integration/coopHaulCargoAdoption.cjs, integration/coopNativeHaulCargo.cjs, docs/UPSTREAM_PATCH_SERIES.md
Evidence: apply-upstream-patches CHECK_OK series=2; tests/coop-haul-cargo-adoption.test.mjs 11/11; found that nativeNpcStore never exported listNativeCargo.

### 2026-10-26T15:06Z · 2db7032 · DOCS · docs(agents): make the L1/L2/L3 layering the first coding rule for agents

Why: agents kept adding string-anchor patches for stock bugs and gameplay alike, which is why the overlay grew fragile.
Where: AGENTS.md, docs/AGENT_START_HERE.md
Evidence: AGENTS.md now opens with "Where a change goes" pointing at docs/UPSTREAM_PATCH_SERIES.md.

### 2026-10-26T15:09Z · 734508c · L1 · refactor(capacitor): replace the L2 top-up hack with the L1 native recharge patch

Why: NPCs got capacitorCapacity but never capacitorRechargeRate, so CCP passive recharge never ran and a strip miner worked once; the overlay topped up the capacitor before every activation instead.
Where: scripts/patch-evejs-coop.cjs, patches/upstream/0002-native-npc-capacitor-recharge.patch, integration/coopNpcCapacitor.cjs (deleted), tests/coop-npc-capacitor.test.mjs (deleted)
Evidence: OVERLAY_OK patched 54 anchorMismatch 0 after L1 apply; native recharge still to be confirmed live.

### 2026-10-26T15:10Z · a85109e · TEST · test(patches): guard the owned upstream series

Why: a patch edited without updating MANIFEST.json, or a reversed pin, would silently change our native state.
Where: tests/upstream-patch-series.test.mjs, .opencode/state.md
Evidence: 4/4 — hash, pin, atomic file list, and "the capacitor hack must stay deleted".

Where: integration/coopDurableCargoWrite.cjs, integration/coopNativeHaulCargo.cjs, tests/coop-durable-cargo-write.test.mjs

Where: AGENTS.md, docs/WORKLOG.md, scripts/log-change.cjs, tests/worklog-format.test.mjs

### 2026-10-26T11:17Z · eed42d6 · L2 · refactor(overlay): split the declarative overlay data out of the patch engine

### 2026-10-26T16:20Z · 421ee96 · L2 · fix(P0): serialize durable depot writers and refuse stale cross-instance journal overwrite

Why: Two createDepot() instances opened the same native depot file while it was empty. Instance A prepared and committed original cargoID 777 (+100 supply). Add exclusive file.lock creation around the disk compare/write/rename window, compare loaded in-memory state with current disk under that lock, poison stale writers instead of overwriting newer receipts, and require explicit operator review to clear abandoned locks after crashes. No automatic unlock of foreign/stale locks.

### 2026-10-26T16:42Z · f17fbae · L2 · fix(P0): cross-instance native warehouse receipt lock through source debit and physical credit

Why: The warehouse 'busy' set only serialized operations inside one JavaScript instance. Exact-source reentrant reproduction used two separate createWarehouse() instances on one journal: during instance A's first persist, instance B completed original cargoID 8001 as COMMITTED and minted exactly one physical ItemStore stack; instance A then overwrote the receipt back to PREPARED and returned SOURCE_DEBIT_UNCERTAIN. The source cargo was gone and target item existed, but the durable history falsely claimed pre-debit. Introduced a per-original-cargoID exclusive .lock created before initial receipt load and held through native debit, ItemStore credit and final journal writes. ### 2026-10-27 · 79365f2 · L2 · fix(P0): preserve foreign crash-recovery temporary journal across native lock races

Why: Both native depot and virtual economy persist used the deterministic temporary name `<journal>.tmp-<process.pid>`, opened it with truncating `"w"`, and always called `unlinkSync(tmp)` on any persist failure. Exact-source prior-main V8 reproduction for both modules: a temp owned by another in-flight/crashed writer with the SAME PID was already present; a writer's lock open returned EEXIST; the catch block removed the foreign temp despite never owning or creating it. This destroys outstanding transaction evidence and can cause uncertainty during recovery. A writer could also truncate an existing same-PID orphan after acquiring the lock. Both persist paths now exclusively create temp with `"wx"`, set an explicit `tmpCreated` ownership flag only after successful open, and cleanup only their own temp. Collision with a pre-existing temp or lock quarantines the failed writer and preserves the foreign file for operator reconciliation. Original disk CAS, native source journal validation, fsync/rename, and lock-release semantics remain.
Where: integration/coopNativeDepot.cjs, integration/coopVirtualEconomy.cjs, tests/coop-native-depot.test.mjs, tests/coop-virtual-economy-native-delivery-durability.test.mjs.

If another worker/crash left a temp with the same name (e.g. PID reuse or rare random collision), the losing worker received EEXIST and erased the foreign custody evidence. Track temp ownership strictly after successful exclusive open, remove only owned incomplete files on failure, and relinquish temp ownership after rename. Keep collision evidence visible to warehouse audit, with no native source debit or item grant.

### 2026-10-27 · 6139d4a · L2 · fix(P0): hold warehouse original-cargo lock throughout repeat adoption

Why: The second-restart path audited warehouse receipts, ItemStore and depot before reparenting npcCargo but did not hold the same exclusive per-cargo <cargoID>.lock that warehouse deposit/resume uses. A separate warehouse process could enter settlement after the read-only scan and before native upsert/flush, causing duplicate or misowned ore. For opt-in repeated adoption, acquire sorted exclusive original-cargo locks after initial preflight; reacquire all settlement proofs and re-enumerate exact original native rows while locks are held; require the replacement hauler still be empty; hold locks through durable flush, readback and compensation. Reject foreign/abandoned locks, preserve foreign custody evidence, release only locks acquired by this operation. A private WeakSet authenticates the lock claim so exported plan() callers cannot skip foreign locks using a fake Set. An unreleaseable lock after mutation quarantines both fleet and hauler for operator repair. First-time adoption behavior and default-off flags are unchanged.
Where: integration/coopHaulCargoAdoption.cjs, tests/coop-haul-cargo-adoption.test.mjs
Evidence: Node 22 real filesystem standalone extraction of exact lock algorithm: 6/6 PASS (successful exclusive lock+release, EEXIST foreign lock, deterministic multi-source cleanup, fsync EIO, unlink EIO, malformed authority/IDs). Seven integrated module regression scenarios added for races, receipt written after first scan, native source drift, lock held through upsert/flush, failed unlock and a forged plan() lock claim, but NOT_RUN here: runner cannot clone GitHub due DNS. Full pinned original GameStore/SQLite SIGKILL WAL/FULL recovery, Rust and two authenticated clients NOT_RUN; no live cargo/ISK, deployment, feature flag changes or remote CI triggered.
